Signal sources and reasons:
- `signal_source: "score_gap"`, `reason: "det_higher"` → could be death or non-death (need to check internal_final for -100)

- `signal_source: "tied_alive"`, `reason: "tie"` → both alive, tied
- `signal_source: "both_dead"`, `reason: "both_died"` → both dead

### User

_source line 90 · 2026-05-23T04:25:57.026Z_

SSH to server westd-18266 (account=root) and read trajectory data from /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/plan_c/find-non-living-thing/episodes.jsonl.

This file has full episode data with step-by-step actions. I need trajectory data for these 20 specific episodes (all from find-non-living-thing):

Det-preferred episodes: 0, 3, 10, 12, 13, 16, 18, 20, 21, 26, 28, 29, 31, 34, 36, 39
Int-preferred episodes: 4, 7, 15, 17

For each episode, extract:
1. The key scoring action from always_internal (the step where score first increased, what object was focused on)
2. The key scoring action from always_deterministic (the step where score first increased, what object was focused on)
3. Any notable differences in behavior (wasted steps, repeated actions, etc.)

The task is "find-non-living-thing" which requires finding and focusing on a non-living thing in ScienceWorld. The scoring depends on what object is found:
- Scores like 50 suggest "picture" (a painting)
- Scores like 58 suggest "orange" (technically non-living when focused as an object)
- Scores like 67 suggest a higher-value non-living thing
- Score 17 suggests a lower-value object
- Score 8 suggests a very low-value object
- Score 0 means no valid object was found

Read the episodes.jsonl file and provide the action sequences for these episodes. Focus on identifying:
- What object each strategy found
- How many steps it took to find it
- How many wasted/repeated/nonsensical actions there were

Use ssh_read_only to read the file. The file is large, so try to grep or use head/tail strategically. Each line is one episode as JSON.

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_read_only · `toolu_01UNSmDrDUVujBV78HjTdBWr`</summary>

```json
{
  "server": "westd-18266",
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport json\ntarget_eps = [0,3,4,7,10,12,13,15,16,17,18,20,21,26,28,29,31,34,36,39]\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/plan_c/find-non-living-thing/episodes.jsonl') as f:\n    for line in f:\n        ep = json.loads(line)\n        idx = ep['episode_idx']\n        if idx not in target_eps:\n            continue\n        ai = ep['always_internal']\n        ad = ep['always_deterministic']\n        # Find first scoring step for each\n        ai_first = None\n        for s in ai['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0:\n                ai_first = s\n                break\n        ad_first = None\n        for s in ad['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0:\n                ad_first = s\n                break\n        print(f'EP {idx} | AI score={ai[\\\"final_score\\\"]} steps={ai[\\\"num_steps\\\"]} | AD score={ad[\\\"final_score\\\"]} steps={ad[\\\"num_steps\\\"]}')\n        if ai_first:\n            print(f'  AI first score: step {ai_first[\\\"step\\\"]} action=\\\"{ai_first[\\\"action\\\"]}\\\" delta={ai_first[\\\"score_delta\\\"]}')\n        else:\n            print(f'  AI first score: NONE (score=0)')\n        if ad_first:\n            print(f'  AD first score: step {ad_first[\\\"step\\\"]} action=\\\"{ad_first[\\\"action\\\"]}\\\" delta={ad_first[\\\"score_delta\\\"]}')\n        else:\n            print(f'  AD first score: NONE (score=0)')\n        print()\n\"\n",
  "account": "root"
}
```

{
  "server": "westd-18266",
  "command": "cat << 'PYEOF' > /tmp/extract_episodes.py\nimport json\n\ntarget_eps = [0,3,4,7,10,12,13,15,16,17,18,20,21,26,28,29,31,34,36,39]\ndet_preferred = [0,3,10,12,13,16,18,20,21,26,28,29,31,34,36,39]\nint_preferred = [4,7,15,17]\n\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/plan_c/find-non-living-thing/episodes.jsonl') as f:\n    for line in f:\n        ep = json.loads(line)\n        idx = ep['episode_idx']\n        if idx not in target_eps:\n            continue\n        ai = ep['always_internal']\n        ad = ep['always_deterministic']\n        \n        # Find first scoring step\n        ai_first = None\n        for s in ai['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0:\n                ai_first = s\n                break\n        ad_first = None\n        for s in ad['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0:\n                ad_first = s\n                break\n        \n        # Count wasted steps after scoring\n        ai_wasted_after = 0\n        ai_scored = False\n        for s in ai['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0 and not ai_scored:\n                ai_scored = True\n                continue\n            if ai_scored:\n                ai_wasted_after += 1\n        \n        ad_wasted_after = 0\n        ad_scored = False\n        for s in ad['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0 and not ad_scored:\n                ad_scored = True\n                continue\n            if ad_scored:\n                ad_wasted_after += 1\n        \n        # Count repeated actions\n        ai_actions = [s['action'] for s in ai['steps']]\n        ad_actions = [s['action'] for s in ad['steps']]\n        ai_repeats = len(ai_actions) - len(set(ai_actions))\n        ad_repeats = len(ad_actions) - len(set(ad_actions))\n        \n        # Count \"connect\" actions (exploration/nonsensical)\n        ai_connects = sum(1 for a in ai_actions if a.startswith('connect'))\n        ad_connects = sum(1 for a in ad_actions if a.startswith('connect'))\n        \n        group = \"DET-PREF\" if idx in det_preferred else \"INT-PREF\"\n        \n        print(f\"===== EP {idx} [{group}] =====\")\n        print(f\"  AI(internal):      score={ai['final_score']:5.1f}  steps={ai['num_steps']:2d}  repeats={ai_repeats}  connects={ai_connects}\")\n        print(f\"  AD(deterministic): score={ad['final_score']:5.1f}  steps={ad['num_steps']:2d}  repeats={ad_repeats}  connects={ad_connects}\")\n        \n        if ai_first:\n            print(f\"  AI first score: step {ai_first['step']:2d}  action=\\\"{ai_first['action']}\\\"  delta={ai_first['score_delta']}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  AI first score: NEVER SCORED\")\n        if ad_first:\n            print(f\"  AD first score: step {ad_first['step']:2d}  action=\\\"{ad_first['action']}\\\"  delta={ad_first['score_delta']}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  AD first score: NEVER SCORED\")\n        \n        if ai_scored:\n            print(f\"  AI wasted steps after scoring: {ai_wasted_after}\")\n        if ad_scored:\n            print(f\"  AD wasted steps after scoring: {ad_wasted_after}\")\n        print()\n\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/extract_episodes.py",
  "account": "root"
}

{
  "server": "westd-18266",
  "command": "cat << 'PYEOF' > /tmp/extract_episodes.py\nimport json\n\ntarget_eps = [0,3,4,7,10,12,13,15,16,17,18,20,21,26,28,29,31,34,36,39]\ndet_preferred = [0,3,10,12,13,16,18,20,21,26,28,29,31,34,36,39]\nint_preferred = [4,7,15,17]\n\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/plan_c/find-non-living-thing/episodes.jsonl') as f:\n    for line in f:\n        ep = json.loads(line)\n        idx = ep['episode_idx']\n        if idx not in target_eps:\n            continue\n        ai = ep['always_internal']\n        ad = ep['always_deterministic']\n        \n        ai_first = None\n        for s in ai['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0:\n                ai_first = s\n                break\n        ad_first = None\n        for s in ad['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0:\n                ad_first = s\n                break\n        \n        ai_wasted_after = 0\n        ai_scored = False\n        for s in ai['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0 and not ai_scored:\n                ai_scored = True\n                continue\n            if ai_scored:\n                ai_wasted_after += 1\n        \n        ad_wasted_after = 0\n        ad_scored = False\n        for s in ad['steps']:\n            if s['score_delta'] > 0 and not ad_scored:\n                ad_scored = True\n                continue\n            if ad_scored:\n                ad_wasted_after += 1\n        \n        ai_actions = [s['action'] for s in ai['steps']]\n        ad_actions = [s['action'] for s in ad['steps']]\n        ai_repeats = len(ai_actions) - len(set(ai_actions))\n        ad_repeats = len(ad_actions) - len(set(ad_actions))\n        \n        ai_connects = sum(1 for a in ai_actions if a.startswith('connect'))\n        ad_connects = sum(1 for a in ad_actions if a.startswith('connect'))\n        \n        group = \"DET-PREF\" if idx in det_preferred else \"INT-PREF\"\n        \n        print(f\"===== EP {idx} [{group}] =====\")\n        print(f\"  AI(internal):      score={ai['final_score']:5.1f}  steps={ai['num_steps']:2d}  repeats={ai_repeats}  connects={ai_connects}\")\n        print(f\"  AD(deterministic): score={ad['final_score']:5.1f}  steps={ad['num_steps']:2d}  repeats={ad_repeats}  connects={ad_connects}\")\n        \n        if ai_first:\n            print(f\"  AI first score: step {ai_first['step']:2d}  action=[{ai_first['action']}]  delta={ai_first['score_delta']}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  AI first score: NEVER SCORED\")\n        if ad_first:\n            print(f\"  AD first score: step {ad_first['step']:2d}  action=[{ad_first['action']}]  delta={ad_first['score_delta']}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  AD first score: NEVER SCORED\")\n        \n        if ai_scored:\n            print(f\"  AI wasted steps after scoring: {ai_wasted_after}\")\n        if ad_scored:\n            print(f\"  AD wasted steps after scoring: {ad_wasted_after}\")\n        print()\nPYEOF\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 /tmp/extract_episodes.py",
  "account": "root"
}
